namespace = toho

event = {
    id = toho.1
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = touhou_fallen_empire_materialist_checked } }
            set_global_flag = touhou_fallen_empire_materialist_checked
            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { has_global_flag = touhou_has_fallen_empire_materialist }
                    any_country = { is_fallen_empire_materialist = yes }
                }
                random_country = {
                    limit = { is_fallen_empire_materialist = yes }
                    set_global_flag = touhou_has_fallen_empire_materialist
                    save_global_event_target_as = touhou_fallen_empire_materialist
                    capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = touhou_fallen_empire_materialist_capital }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = touhou_only_gensokyo_ai_checked } }
            set_global_flag = touhou_only_gensokyo_ai_checked
            if = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { any_country = { is_ai = no has_authority = auth_gensokyo } }
                    any_country = { is_ai = yes has_authority = auth_gensokyo }
                }
                set_global_flag = touhou_has_only_gensokyo_ai
                random_country = {
                    limit = { is_ai = yes has_authority = auth_gensokyo }
                    set_country_flag = touhou_is_choosen_gensokyo_ai
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = touhou_fe_union_titan_country_display } spth_has_ag_mod = yes }
            set_global_flag = touhou_fe_union_titan_country_display
            every_playable_country = {
                limit = { is_ai = no any_owned_ship = { is_ship_size = touhou_fe_union_titan } }
                add_modifier = { modifier = touhou_fe_union_titan_country days = -1 }
            }
        }
    }
}
country_event = {
    id = toho.2
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = { NOT = { has_country_flag = touhou_player_bonus_fired } }
    immediate = {
        set_country_flag = touhou_player_bonus_fired
        touhou_difficulty_bonus_player = yes
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.16
    title = toho.16.name
    desc = toho.16.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_leader_function_ui
    custom_gui_option = spth_leader_function_button
    picture_event_data = {
        room = shining_needle_castle_room
        portrait = event_target:touhou_magical_mallet_user_@this
    }
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { is_variable_set = touhou_magical_mallet_duration } }
            set_variable = { which = touhou_magical_mallet_duration value = 180 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { is_variable_set = touhou_magical_mallet_strength } }
            set_variable = { which = touhou_magical_mallet_strength value = @touhou_magical_mallet_strength_average }
        }
    }
    after = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = touhou_magical_mallet_should_close }
            hidden_effect = {
                remove_country_flag = touhou_magical_mallet_should_close
                remove_country_flag = touhou_magical_mallet_opened
            }
        } else = { country_event = { id = toho.16 } }
    }
    # 调整持续时间
    option = {
        name = BUTTON_TEXT_NULL
        custom_gui = touhou_magical_mallet_duration_button
    }
    # 调整影响程度
    option = {
        name = BUTTON_TEXT_NULL
        custom_gui = touhou_magical_mallet_strength_button
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.16.a
        custom_tooltip = toho.16.a.effect
        allow = { touhou_magical_mallet_check = { type = a } }
        touhou_magical_mallet_fire_effect = { type = a }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.16.b
        custom_tooltip = toho.16.b.effect
        allow = { touhou_magical_mallet_check = { type = b } }
        touhou_magical_mallet_fire_effect = { type = b }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.16.c
        custom_tooltip = toho.16.c.effect
        allow = { touhou_magical_mallet_check = { type = c } }
        touhou_magical_mallet_fire_effect = { type = c save_temp_days = yes delay_event = toho.17 modifier = touhou_magical_mallet_c_modifier base_modifier = pop_bonus_workforce_mult modifier_base = 0.4 modifier_factor = 0.1 }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.16.d
        custom_tooltip = toho.16.d.effect
        allow = { touhou_magical_mallet_check = { type = d } }
        touhou_magical_mallet_fire_effect = { type = d }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.16.e
        custom_tooltip = toho.16.e.effect
        allow = { touhou_magical_mallet_check = { type = e } }
        touhou_magical_mallet_fire_effect = { type = e }
    }
    option = {
        name = TH_EXIT
        default_hide_option = yes
        hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = touhou_magical_mallet_should_close }
    }
}
country_event = {
    id = toho.17
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    immediate = {
        remove_modifier = touhou_magical_mallet_c_modifier
        add_modifier = {
            modifier = touhou_magical_mallet_c_modifier days = 1
            time_multiplier = touhou_magical_mallet_duration_temp_c
            mult = value:touhou_magical_mallet_modifier_value|base|-0.2|factor|-0.05|modifier|pop_bonus_workforce_mult|
        }
    }
}
country_event = {
    id = toho.18
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        NOT = { has_country_flag = has_given_lunar_capital_carrier }
        has_authority = auth_gensokyo is_ai = no
        has_technology = tech_lunar_capital_ships
    }
    immediate = {
        set_country_flag = has_given_lunar_capital_carrier
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_existing_ship_design = lunar_capital_carrier } }
            create_ship_design = { design = NAME_lunar_capital_carrier_player }
            add_ship_design = last_created_design
        }
    }
}
country_event = {
    id = toho.19
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = { has_authority = auth_gensokyo has_country_flag = th_kosuzu_get }
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:touhou_kosuzu_@root } }
            random_owned_leader = {
                limit = { spth_is_touhou_gensokyo_leader = yes has_trait = leader_trait_kosuzu }
                save_global_event_target_as = touhou_kosuzu_@root
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { exists = event_target:touhou_kosuzu_@root }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    is_variable_set = touhou_kosuzu_skill
                    check_variable_arithmetic = {
                        which = touhou_kosuzu_skill
                        value < value:touhou_kosuzu_skill_value
                    }
                }
                set_variable = { which = touhou_kosuzu_skill value = value:touhou_kosuzu_skill_value }
                if = {
                    limit = { has_modifier = country_building_suzunaan_modifier }
                    remove_modifier = country_building_suzunaan_modifier
                }
                add_modifier = { modifier = country_building_suzunaan_modifier mult = touhou_kosuzu_skill }
            } else = {
                set_variable = { which = touhou_kosuzu_skill value = value:touhou_kosuzu_skill_value }
            }
        }
    }
}
country_event = {
    id = toho.20
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        can_access_shroud = yes
    }
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { is_last_increased_tech_category = spirit_power }
            add_patron_objective_counter = {
                counter = po_research_touhou_tech
                amount = 1
            }
        }
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.40
    title = toho.40.name
    desc = toho.40.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_arena_room }
    option = {
        name = TH_OK
        allow = {
            resource_stockpile_compare = {
                resource = food
                value > 1000
                mult = trigger:num_owned_planets
            }
            resource_stockpile_compare = {
                resource = sr_lingli
                value > 100
                mult = trigger:num_owned_planets
            }
            resource_stockpile_compare = {
                resource = trade
                value > 250
                mult = trigger:num_owned_planets
            }
        }
        add_resource = {
            food = -1000
            sr_lingli = -100
            trade = -250
            mult = trigger:num_owned_planets
        }
        country_event = { id = toho.41 days = 60 random = 60 }
    }
    option = {
        name = TH_WAIT
    }
}

country_event = {
	id = toho.30
	title = "toho.30.name"
	desc = {
		text = toho.30.desc
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_hm3
	show_sound = event_radio_chatter
	location = capital_scope
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = toho.30.a
		capital_scope = {
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "HM3_PROJECT"
				location = this
				owner = root
			}
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = toho.31
	title = "toho.31.name"
	desc = "toho.31.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_hm3
	show_sound = event_synthetic_evolution
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = touhou_special_ship
		give_technology = {
			tech = tech_bio_reactor
			message = no
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_same_species = root
				NOT = {
					species = {
						has_trait = trait_vampire_1
					}
				}
			}
			modify_species = {
				species = this
				add_trait = trait_vampire_1
			}
			species = {
				save_event_target_as = vampire_species
			}
		}
		change_dominant_species = {
			species = event_target:vampire_species
			change_all = yes
		}
		every_owned_ship = {
			limit = {
				is_ship_class = shipclass_colonizer
				species = {
				}
			}
			change_species = event_target:vampire_species
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_vampire_1
					has_trait = trait_touhou
				}
			}
			change_species = event_target:vampire_species
			change_leader_portrait = species
		}
		change_dominant_species = {
			species = event_target:vampire_species
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				species = {
					has_trait = trait_vampire_1
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_vampire_1
				}
			}
			spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_vampire_1 }
			remove_trait = leader_trait_cyborg
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			limit = {
				species = {
					has_trait = trait_vampire_1
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_vampire_1
				}
			}
			spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_vampire_1 }
			remove_trait = leader_trait_cyborg
		}
		#observer_event = {
		#	id = observer.toho3
		#}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				NOT = {
					is_same_value = root
				}
				has_communications = root
			}
			country_event = {
				id = toho.33
				days = 5
			}
			country_event = {
				id = toho.34
				days = 10
			}
		}
		ROOT = {
			set_country_flag = vampire_age
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "toho.31.a"
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = toho.36
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = toho.35
	title = "toho.31.name"
	desc = "toho.31.desc"
	picture = GFX_evt_hm3
	show_sound = event_hm_zhys
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				species = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = trait_touhou
					}
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
				}
				75 = {
					spth_kill_pop_group = yes
				}
			}
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				species = {
					NOR = {
						has_trait = trait_vampire_1
						has_trait = trait_touhou
					}
				}
			}
			change_species = owner_species
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = {
				is_same_species = root
				NOT = {
					species = {
						has_trait = trait_vampire_1
					}
				}
			}
			modify_species = {
				species = this
				add_trait = trait_vampire_1
			}
			species = {
				save_event_target_as = vampire_species
			}
		}
		change_dominant_species = {
			species = event_target:vampire_species
			change_all = yes
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_vampire_1
				}
			}
			change_species = owner_species
			change_leader_portrait = species
			set_skill = 10
		}
		every_pool_leader = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_vampire_1
				}
			}
			change_species = owner_species
			change_leader_portrait = species
			set_skill = 10
		}
		every_owned_leader = {
			limit = {
				species = {
					has_trait = trait_vampire_1
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = leader_trait_vampire_1
				}
			}
			spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_vampire_1 }
			remove_trait = leader_trait_cyborg
			set_skill = 10
		}

			limit = {
				species = {
					has_trait = trait_vampire_1
				}
				NOR = {

				}
			}
			spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_vampire_1 }
			remove_trait = leader_trait_cyborg
			set_skill = 10
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = toho.36
				days = 1800
			}
		}
	}
    option = { name = GOOD }
}

	id = toho.38
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		from = {
			species = { has_trait = trait_touhou }
			NOT = { has_leader_flag = own_th_leader_trait }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		from = {
			if = {
				limit = { spth_is_touhou_gensokyo_leader = no }
				set_leader_flag = own_th_leader_trait
				random_list = {
					20 = {
						spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_th_witch }
					}
					20 = {
						spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_th_immortal }
					}
					10 = {
						spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_th_kami }
					}
					40 = {
						spth_add_trait = { trait = leader_trait_th_yousei }
					}
					20 = {
						###空###
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		from = {
			leader_event = {
				days = 1
				id = toho.39
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = toho.52
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		every_owned_planet = {
			limit = {
				is_colony = yes
				any_owned_pop_group = {
					NOT = { pop_group_has_trait = trait_touhou }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { which = touhou_convert_counter value = 0 }
			every_owned_pop_group = {
				limit = { NOT = { pop_group_has_trait = trait_touhou } }
				random_list = {
					1 = { change_variable = { which = touhou_convert_counter value = 1 } }
					9 = {  }
				}
				reroll_random = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = touhou_convert_counter value < 1 } }
				set_variable = { which = touhou_convert_counter value = 1 }
			}
			while = { count = touhou_convert_counter create_pop_group = { species = root.owner_main_species } }
			clear_variable = touhou_convert_counter
		}
		every_owned_pop_group = {
			limit = { NOT = { pop_group_has_trait = trait_touhou } }
			spth_kill_pop_group = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = toho.51
				days = 1800
			}
		}
	}
}

#设置领袖
country_event = { # 红魔馆势力
	id = toho.50
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = toho_select_ruler_scarlet
		set_variable = { which = spth_gal_portrait_character value = @spth_gal_portrait_remilia }
		set_variable_to_random_value = { which = spth_gal_remilia_talk min = 0 max = 4 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { exists = event_target:spth_gal_country_remilia }
			}
			create_country = {
				name = NAME_spth_gal_remilia
				type = spth_gal_remilia
				flag = root
				species = root
				effect = {
					save_global_event_target_as = spth_gal_country_remilia
					set_country_flag = protected_from_queen_storm
				}
			}
		}
		# establish_communications_no_message = event_target:spth_gal_country_remilia
		# country_event = { id = spth_leader_gal.200 }
        spth_effect_init_container_country_effect = yes
		spth_effect_init_meirin_effect = yes
		spth_effect_init_patchouli_effect = yes
		spth_effect_init_sakuya_effect = yes
        spth_effect_init_flandre_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_civic = civic_fight_little_devil
				}
			}
            spth_effect_init_koakuma_effect = yes
		}
        change_variable = {
            which = spth_card_current_leader
            value = 5
        }
        spth_effect_init_remilia_effect = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = toho.8001
	title = toho.8001.name
	desc = toho.8001.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_th_terakoya_1
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
	custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
	picture_event_data = { room = th_terakoya_1_room }

	trigger = {
		has_authority = auth_gensokyo
		NOT = { has_country_flag = th_terakoya_reset }
		has_technology = tech_terakoya_reset
	}

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = th_terakoya_reset
	}

	option = {
		name = toho.8001.aa
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = {
				which = th_textbook_edict_num
				value = 0
			}
		}
		capital_scope = {
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "th_terakoya_books_edict_history"
				location = this
				owner = root
			}
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "th_terakoya_books_edict_math"
				location = this
				owner = root
			}
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "th_terakoya_books_edict_nature"
				location = this
				owner = root
			}
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "th_terakoya_books_edict_agriculture"
				location = this
				owner = root
			}
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "th_terakoya_books_edict_writing"
				location = this
				owner = root
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = toho.8001.bb
		add_resource = {
			influence = 100
		}
		hidden_effect = {

				modifier = th_terakoya_teachers
			}
		}
	}

# fumo fumo!
event = {
	id = toho.8008
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = toho_8008_fired } }
	immediate = {
		random_playable_country = {
			limit = { is_ai = no spth_check_gensokyo_auth = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						is_ai = yes is_country_type = default
						spth_check_gensokyo_auth = no
						has_communications = prev
						opinion_level = { who = prev level >= good }
					}
				}
				set_global_flag = toho_8008_fired
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = yes is_country_type = default
						spth_check_gensokyo_auth = no
						has_communications = prev
						opinion_level = { who = prev level >= good }
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = toho_8008_target
				}
				country_event = {
					id = toho.8009
					scopes = {
						from = event_target:toho_8008_target
						fromfrom = event_target:toho_8008_target.capital_scope
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.8080
    title = toho.8080.name
    desc = {
        text = toho.8080.desc
        trigger = { NOT = { has_origin = origin_spth_story_gensokyo } }
    }
    desc = {
        text = toho.8080.desc.lunar
        trigger = { has_origin = origin_spth_story_gensokyo }
    }
    trigger = {
        has_authority = auth_gensokyo
    }
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = EMPTY_STRING
    custom_gui = spth_special_diplomacy_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_special_diplomacy_option
    force_open = yes
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room portrait = shroud1 }
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:spth_story_lunar_capital_area_toyohime } }
            set_global_flag = spth_story_lunar_capital_toyohime_spawned
            random_system = {
                set_spawn_system_batch = begin
                spawn_system = {
                    initializer = spth_story_lunar_capital_area_toyohime
                    hyperlane = no
                }
                set_spawn_system_batch = end
                random_system_planet = { save_event_target_as = spth_story_lunar_capital_aethon_planet_1 }
                spawn_megastructure = {
                    type = spth_lunar_jumpdrive_gateway
                    coords_from = event_target:spth_story_lunar_capital_aethon_planet_1
                    owner = root
                    orbit_distance = 0
                    orbit_angle = 0
                    graphical_culture = root
                    init_effect = {
                        set_megastructure_flag = is_spth_lunar_jumpdrive_gateway
                        set_megastructure_flag = spth_lunar_jumpdrive_gateway_debug
                    }
                }
                event_target:spth_story_lunar_capital_area_toyohime = {
                    set_star_flag = spth_debug_spawn
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = spth_lunar_capital_psi_crisis_begin } }
            set_global_flag = spth_lunar_capital_psi_crisis_begin
            set_global_flag = lunar_jumpdrive_gateway_blocked
            touhou_destory_crisis_shpere = yes
            spth_lunar_psi_crisis_setup_sub_gate = { spth_id = 1 }
            spth_lunar_psi_crisis_setup_sub_gate = { spth_id = 2 }
            spth_lunar_psi_create_country = yes
            event_target:spth_story_lunar_capital_area_toyohime = {
                spth_lunar_psi_spawn_starbase = yes
            }
            while = { count = 5 spth_lunar_psi_create_defence_fleet = yes }
            spth_lunar_psi_create_gate = { spth_id = 0 }
            every_playable_country = {
                country_event = { id = lunar_capital_psi.150 }
                country_event = { id = lunar_capital_psi.35 }
            }
        }
    }
    show_sound = event_shroud_song
    option = { name = toho.8080.a is_dialog_only = yes response_text = toho.8080.a.response }
    option = { name = toho.8080.b is_dialog_only = yes response_text = toho.8080.b.response }
    option = { name = toho.8080.c response_text = toho.8080.c.response }
    option = { name = toho.8080.d response_text = toho.8080.d.response default_hide_option = yes }
    after = {
        custom_tooltip = toho.8080.tooltip
    }
}

# gatekeeper
country_event = {
	id = toho.8090
	hide_window = yes
	mean_time_to_happen = { years = 6 }
	trigger = { spth_check_gensokyo_auth = yes years_passed >= 25 }
	immediate = {
		reroll_random = yes
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { has_country_flag = toho_8100_fired NOT = { has_country_flag = th_terakoya_reset } } }
				set_country_flag = toho_8100_fired
				country_event = { id = toho.8100 days = 30 random = 30 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = toho_8102_fired }
				set_country_flag = toho_8102_fired
				country_event = { id = toho.8102 days = 30 random = 30 }
			}
            10 = {
                modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = toho_8091_fired }
                modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { has_country_flag = th_eirin_get } }
                set_country_flag = toho_8091_fired
                country_event = { id = toho.8091 days = 15 random = 15 }
            }
            10 = {
                modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = toho_8099_fired }
                modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { has_country_flag = th_aya_get } }
                modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { has_country_flag = th_eirin_get } }
                modifier = { add = 5 has_country_flag = th_eirin_get }
                modifier = { add = 5 has_country_flag = th_yukari_get }
                country_event = { id = toho.8099 }
            }
            10 = {
                modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = toho_8117_fired }
                country_event = { id = toho.8117 days = 30 random = 30 }
            }
            10 = {
                modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = th_leader_sp_2_fired }
                country_event = { id = th_leader_sp.2 days = 30 random = 30 }
            }
            10 = {
                modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = th_boss_100_fired }
                modifier = { factor = 0 years_passed <= 35 }
                country_event = { id = th_boss.100 days = 30 random = 90 }
            }
            10 = {
                modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = toho_4227_pre_fired }
                modifier = {
                    factor = 0
                    NOT = { has_country_flag = th_shinki_get }
                }
                modifier = {
                    factor = 0
                    NOT = { has_country_flag = spth_precursor_makai_ruined_ship_get }
                }
                modifier = {
                    factor = 0
                    NOT = { exists = event_target:spth_makai_ship_core_unlocked_player }
                }
                set_country_flag = toho_4227_pre_fired
                country_event = { id = toho.4227 days = 30 random = 60 }
            }
			30 = { log = "\\[This.GetName]无事发生QAQ" }
		}
	}
}

    id = toho.8091
    title = toho.8091.name
    desc = toho.8091.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
    picture_event_data = { room = eientei_room }
    location = capital_scope
    option = {
        name = toho.8091.a
        default_hide_option = yes
        enable_special_project = { name = touhou_research_gunpower_turret owner = root }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.8091.b
    }
}

    }
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.8095
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        NOT = { has_country_flag = toho_8094_fired }
        has_technology = tech_touhou_fe_union_titan
        has_technology = tech_touhou_gunpower_turret
        has_country_flag = th_eirin_get
        years_passed >= 40
    }
    immediate = {
        set_country_flag = toho_8094_fired
        country_event = { id = toho.8094 days = 30 random = 30 }
    }
}

# 秦心
country_event = {
	id = toho.8102
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		random_owned_planet = {
			limit = { is_colony = yes has_fully_upgraded_capital = yes }
			save_event_target_as = toho_kokoro_planet
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:toho_kokoro_planet } }
			random_owned_planet = { limit = { is_colony = yes } save_event_target_as = toho_kokoro_planet }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { exists = event_target:toho_kokoro_planet }
			# 吗的你没有球？开摆了
			event_target:toho_kokoro_planet = { set_planet_flag = toho_kokoro_planet_@root }
			country_event = { id = toho.8103 scopes = { from = event_target:toho_kokoro_planet } }

		}
	}
}
country_event = {
	id = toho.8103
	title = toho.8103.name
	desc = toho.8103.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
	custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
	picture_event_data = { room = th135_human_village_night_room }
	option = { name = toho.8103.a }
	after = {
		from = {
			add_modifier = { modifier = touhou_kokoro_planet_modifier_1 }
			save_global_event_target_as = toho_8103_target_@root
		}
		enable_special_project = {
			name = touhou_kokoro_survey_project
			location = from owner = this
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

# 幻想乡小超主
country_event = {
    id = toho.8200
    title = toho.8200.name
    desc = toho.8200.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = touhou_shroud_lower_level
    custom_gui = spth_special_diplomacy_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_special_diplomacy_option
    force_open = yes
    picture_event_data = {
        room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room
        portrait = shroud1
    }
    show_sound = {
        trigger = { is_robot_empire = yes }
        sound = event_psionic_robot
    }
    show_sound = {
        trigger = { is_robot_empire = no }
        sound = event_psionic
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.8200.a
        default_hide_option = yes
        enable_patron_first_contact_project = {
            name = TOUHOU_PSIONIC_ECHO_SANAE_FC_PROJECT
            patron = touhou_psionic_echo_sanae
            owner = this
        }
    }
}
country_event = {
    id = toho.8201
    title = toho.8201.name
    desc = toho.8201.desc.intro
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room }
    option = {
        name = toho.8201.a
        default_hide_option = yes
        tooltip = {
            set_patron_first_contact_state = {
                patron = touhou_psionic_echo_sanae
                state = completed
            }
        }
        add_monthly_resource_mult = {
            resource = sr_zro
            value = @tier5materialreward
            min = @tier5materialmin
            max = @tier5materialmax
        }
        add_monthly_resource_mult = {
            resource = sr_lingli
            value = @tier5materialreward
            min = @tier5materialmin
            max = @tier5materialmax
        }
    }
    immediate = {
        set_patron_first_contact_state = {
            patron = touhou_psionic_echo_sanae
            state = completed
        }
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.8210
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        NOT = { has_country_flag = toho_8210_fired }
        has_authority = auth_gensokyo
        touhou_has_psionic_advance_auth = yes
        OR = {
            has_country_flag = toho_select_ruler_sanae
            has_country_flag = toho_select_ruler_reimu
            has_country_flag = toho_select_ruler_scarlet
            has_country_flag = toho_select_ruler_yuyuko
            has_country_flag = toho_select_ruler_satori
        }
        exists = ruler ruler = {
            is_touhou_special_leader = yes
            has_trait = leader_trait_touhou_psionic
        }
    }
    immediate = {
        set_country_flag = toho_8210_fired
        country_event = { id = toho.8211 days = 30 random = 45 }
    }
}

# 时尚小垃圾
country_event = {
	id = toho.8108
	title = toho.8108.name
	desc = toho.8108.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	location = event_target:spth_rw_system
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
	custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
	picture_event_data = { room = spth_ringworld_room }
	option = { name = TH_ACKNOWLEDGED }
}
# From = Megastructure
# FromFrom = System
country_event = {
	id = toho.8109
	title = toho.8109.name
	desc = toho.8109.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	location = fromfrom
	trigger = {
		from = {
			OR = {
				is_megastructure_type = spth_story_ring_world_3_gensokyo
				is_megastructure_type = spth_story_ring_world_3_common
			}
		}
		from = {
			NOT = {
				any_system_megastructure = {
					OR = {
						is_megastructure_type = spth_story_ring_world_2_gensokyo
						is_megastructure_type = spth_story_ring_world_2_common
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = { fromfrom = { save_event_target_as = spth_rw_system } }
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
	custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
	picture_event_data = { room = spth_ringworld_room }
	option = { name = TH_ACKNOWLEDGED }
}

first_contact_event = {
	id = toho.8113
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		contact_country = { is_same_value = event_target:amoeba_country  } exists = owner
		owner = {
			OR = { has_valid_civic = civic_chireiden has_country_flag = th_satori_get }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = touhou_satori_amoeba_fired has_special_project = AMOEBAS_PACIFICATION_PROJECT }
			has_communications = root.contact_country
			is_hostile = root.contact_country
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		log = "toho.8113 fired."
		owner = { country_event = { id = toho.8115 days = 15 random = 15 } }
	}
}
country_event = {
	id = toho.8114
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_country_flag = toho_8114_fired }
		OR = { has_valid_civic = civic_chireiden has_country_flag = th_satori_get }
		exists = event_target:amoeba_country
		has_communications = event_target:amoeba_country
		is_hostile = event_target:amoeba_country
	}
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = toho_8114_fired
		country_event = { id = toho.8115 days = 30 random = 45 }
	}
}
country_event = {
	id = toho.8115
	title = toho.8115.name
	desc = toho.8115.desc
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = touhou_satori_amoeba_fired
			has_special_project = AMOEBAS_PACIFICATION_PROJECT
		}
		OR = { has_valid_civic = civic_chireiden has_country_flag = th_satori_get }
		exists = event_target:amoeba_country
		has_communications = event_target:amoeba_country
		is_hostile = event_target:amoeba_country
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
	custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
	picture_event_data = { room = th_satori_1_room }
	immediate = { set_country_flag = touhou_satori_amoeba_fired }
	option = {
		name = toho.8115.a
		default_hide_option = yes
		capital_scope = {
			enable_special_project = {
				name = "AMOEBAS_PACIFICATION_PROJECT"
				location = this
				owner = root.owner
			}
		}
	}
}
fleet_event = {
	id = toho.8116
	title = toho.8116.name
	desc = toho.8116.desc
	trigger = {
		exists = from from = { has_ship_flag = raging is_growth_complete = no }
		fromfrom = { has_menace_perk = menp_behemoth_mind_meld }
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
	custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
	picture_event_data = { room = th_satori_1_room }
	option = {
		name = toho.8116.a
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	option = {
		name = toho.8116.b
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

            country_event = { id = toho.8999 }      # 虚境受抑制

            limit = { touhou_is_shroud_excited = yes }
            country_event = { id = toho.8301 }      # 虚境活跃

            limit = { touhou_is_shroud_inhibited = yes }
            country_event = { id = toho.8997 }      # 虚境受抑制
        } else = {
            log = "into to toho common shroud."

                            OR = {

                            }

    id = toho.8302
    title = toho.8302.name
    desc = toho.8302.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = BLANK_STRING
    custom_gui = spth_special_diplomacy_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_special_diplomacy_option
    force_open = yes
    picture_event_data = { portrait = shroud1 room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room }
    show_sound = event_psionic
    option = {
        name = toho.8302.a
        default_hide_option = yes
        enable_patron_first_contact_project = {
            name = TOUHOU_PSIONIC_ECHO_KEIEI_FC_PROJECT
            patron = touhou_psionic_echo_keiei
            owner = this
        }
    }
}

                        show_notification = no

            remove_global_flag = synth_queen_ongoing
            set_global_flag = synth_queen_defeated

    title = "toho.8301.name"
    desc = "utopia.3160.desc"
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = BLANK_STRING
    custom_gui = spth_special_diplomacy_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_special_diplomacy_option
    force_open = yes
    picture_event_data = { portrait = shroud1 room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room }
    immediate = { set_country_flag = shroud_diplomacy_engaged }
    after = { hidden_effect = { remove_country_flag = shroud_diplomacy_engaged } }
    option = {
        name = "utopia.3160.a"
        custom_tooltip = "shroud.unknown.chances.medium"
        hidden_effect = {
            reroll_random = yes
            random_list = {
                60 = { country_event = { id = toho.8401 } }
                40 = {
                    modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_relic = r_zro_crystal }
                    country_event = { id = toho.8402 }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = "exit.shroud"
        custom_tooltip = "exit.shroud.tooltip"
        default_hide_option = yes
    }
}
# 成功
country_event = {
    id = toho.8401
    title = "utopia.3161.name"
    desc = "utopia.3161.desc"
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = BLANK_STRING
    custom_gui = spth_special_diplomacy_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_special_diplomacy_option
    force_open = yes
    picture_event_data = { portrait = shroud1 room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room }
    immediate = { set_country_flag = shroud_diplomacy_engaged }
    after = {
        hidden_effect = {
            remove_country_flag = shroud_diplomacy_engaged
            set_timed_country_flag = { flag = positive_shroud_outcome days = 1800 }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = "exit.shroud"
        default_hide_option = yes
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOR = {
                    has_technology = tech_psi_jump_drive_1
                    has_tech_option = tech_psi_jump_drive_1
                }
            }
            add_research_option = tech_psi_jump_drive_1
            add_tech_progress = { tech = tech_psi_jump_drive_1 progress = 0.5 }
        }
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.8410
    title = "toho.8410.name"
    desc = "toho.8410.desc"
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = BLANK_STRING
    custom_gui = spth_special_diplomacy_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_special_diplomacy_option
    force_open = yes
    picture_event_data = { portrait = shroud1 room = spth_ag_excited_shroud_room }
    immediate = {
        set_country_flag = shroud_diplomacy_engaged
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_origin = origin_spth_story_gensokyo } }
            if = {
                limit = { has_civic = civic_gensokyo }
                set_country_flag = tech_th_shield_barrier_psionic
            } else = {
                set_country_flag = tech_th_armor_scarlet_psionic
            }
        } else = {
            set_country_flag = tech_th_shield_barrier_psionic_story
            set_country_flag = tech_th_armor_barrier_psionic_story
        }
    }
    after = {
        hidden_effect = {
            remove_country_flag = shroud_diplomacy_engaged
            set_timed_country_flag = { flag = positive_shroud_outcome days = 1800 }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = "exit.shroud"
        default_hide_option = yes
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_origin = origin_spth_story_gensokyo } }
            if = {
                limit = { has_civic = civic_gensokyo }
                add_research_option = tech_th_shield_barrier_psionic
            } else = {
                add_research_option = tech_th_armor_scarlet_psionic
            }
        } else = {
            add_research_option = tech_th_shield_barrier_psionic_story
            add_research_option = tech_th_armor_barrier_psionic_story
        }
    }
}

planet_event = {
	id = toho.9000
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = kamikakushi_planet
		set_variable = { which = kamikakushi_pop_num value = 0 }
		export_trigger_value_to_variable = {
			trigger = spth_pop_group_amount
			variable = kamikakushi_pop_num
		}
		from.owner = {
			set_variable = {
				which = kamikakushi_num
				value = root.kamikakushi_pop_num
			}
			divide_variable = {
				which = kamikakushi_num
				value = 5
			}
			set_variable = {
				which = kamikakushi_num_1
				value = kamikakushi_num
			}
		}
		clear_variable = kamikakushi_pop_num
		spth_planet_kill_all_pop = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = owner
				}
				owner = {
					if = {
						limit = { #isn't the case when the crisis bombards planets, for instance
							is_at_war_with = from.fleet.owner
						}
						add_static_war_exhaustion = {
							attacker = from.fleet.owner
							location = root
							value_for_planet_destruction = 1.0
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_observation_outpost = yes
				}
				observation_outpost_owner = {
					country_event = { id = planet_destruction.1000 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_capital = yes }
				from.owner = { set_country_flag = exterminatus }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = owner
					owner = {
						NOT = { is_same_value = from.owner }
						OR = {
							is_country_type = default
							is_country_type = fallen_empire
							is_country_type = awakened_fallen_empire
						}
					}
				}
				# Generate threat
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_colony = yes
					}
					add_threat = { who = from.owner amount = 3 }
					# modifier for allies + those upset by genocide
					every_country = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								is_same_value = from.owner
								is_same_value = root.owner
								AND = {
									has_federation = yes
									is_in_federation_with = from.owner
								}
							}
							OR = {
								has_communications = from.owner
								has_communications = root.owner
							}
							OR = {
								AND = {
									has_federation = yes
									is_in_federation_with = root.owner
								}
								has_ai_personality = awakened_fallen_empire_xenophile
								AND = {
									is_country_type = default
									OR = {
										is_egalitarian = yes
										is_xenophile = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = { root = { is_colony = no } }
							add_opinion_modifier = {
								modifier = opinion_cracked_an_uninhabited_world
								who = from.owner
							}
						}
						else = {
							add_opinion_modifier = {
								modifier = opinion_cracked_a_world
								who = from.owner
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_threat = { who = from.owner amount = 1 }
				}
				# modifiers for victim
				owner = {
					if = {
						limit = { root = { is_colony = no } }
						add_opinion_modifier = {
							modifier = opinion_kamikakushi
							who = from.owner
						}
					}
					else = {
						add_opinion_modifier = {
							modifier = opinion_kamikakushi
							who = from.owner
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness } }
						add_modifier = {
							modifier = colossus_victim
							days = 10800 # 30 years
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					from.owner = {
						has_country_flag = kamikakushi_used
					}
				}
				from.owner = {
					country_event = {
						id = toho.9002
					}
				}
				else = {
					from.owner = {
						country_event = {
							id = toho.9001
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = toho.3002
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = spth_moon_barrier_finished
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_chain = spth_event_chain_past_legacy }
			add_event_chain_counter = {
				event_chain = spth_event_chain_past_legacy
				counter = spth_past_legacy_ruins_find
				amount = 1
			}
			create_point_of_interest = {
				id = spth_past_legacy_poi.1
				name = spth_past_legacy_poi.1
				desc = spth_past_legacy_poi.1.desc
				event_chain = spth_event_chain_past_legacy
				location = event_target:spth_story_sol_system_lunar_capital
			}
		}
		switch = {
			trigger = has_country_flag
			spth_break_moon_barrier = {
				event_target:spth_story_sol_system_lunar_capital = {
					set_planet_entity = {
						entity = city_planet_01_entity
						graphical_culture = owner
					}
					create_ambient_object = {
						type = "th_gensokyo_mofazhen_object"
						entity_offset_height = { min = -35 max = -40 }
						location = this
					}
					last_created_ambient_object = {
						set_location = { target = prev }
					}
					set_planet_flag = spth_fixed_lunar
					set_planet_flag = spth_lunar_break_barrier
					remove_modifier = spth_lunar_double
					add_modifier = { modifier = spth_lunar_break_barrier }
					add_modifier = { modifier = paradise_made }
					create_message = {
						type = spth_break_lunar_barrier
						localization = spth_message_break_lunar_barrier_desc
						days = 32
						target = this
					}
				}
			}
			spth_repair_moon_barrier = {
				event_target:spth_story_sol_system_lunar_capital = {
					create_ambient_object = {
						type = "th_gensokyo_mofazhen_object"
						entity_offset_height = { min = -35 max = -40 }
						location = this
					}
					last_created_ambient_object = {
						set_location = { target = prev }
					}
					set_planet_flag = spth_fixed_lunar
					set_planet_flag = spth_lunar_repair_barrier
					remove_modifier = spth_lunar_double
					add_modifier = { modifier = spth_lunar_repair_barrier }
					add_modifier = { modifier = paradise_planet }
					create_message = {
						type = spth_repair_lunar_barrier
						localization = spth_message_repair_lunar_barrier_desc
						days = 32
						target = this
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = toho.3008
	title = toho.3008
	desc = toho.3008.desc
	picture = GFX_evt_th_arc_mifeng

			has_country_flag = spth_torifune_monster_flag
		}
		fromfrom = {
			has_planet_flag = spth_story_sol_system_torifune
			has_planet_flag = controlled_by_torifune_monster
		}
	}

		fromfrom = {
			remove_planet_flag = controlled_by_torifune_monster
			set_planet_flag = spth_clear_torifune_monster

			set_owner = root
			reroll_deposits = yes
			reroll_planet_modifiers = yes
			add_deposit = d_spth_torifune_ruin
			add_deposit = d_spth_torifune_ruin
			add_deposit = d_spth_torifune_ruin

		}
		event_target:spth_torifune_monster = { destroy_country = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = toho.3008.a
	}
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.4008
    title = toho.4008.name
    desc = toho.4008.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    diplomatic_title = BLANK_STRING
    custom_gui = touhou_gateway_config_window
    custom_gui_option = touhou_gateway_config_option
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_es2_aethon_room }
    show_sound = event_mystic_reveal
    trigger = { exists = event_target:touhou_gateway_network_current_system }
    after = {
        remove_global_flag = touhou_gateway_network_master_node_configure_opened
        clear_global_event_target = touhou_gateway_network_current_system
    }
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller } }
            save_global_event_target_as = touhou_gateway_network_controller
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller = {
                    NOR = {
                        has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_whitelist
                        has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_blacklist
                    }
                }
            }
            event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller = {
                set_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_blacklist
            }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = "BUTTON_TEXT_NULL"
        is_dialog_only = yes

        custom_gui = touhou_gateway_config_quantum_catapult
    }

country_event = {
    id = toho.4010
    title = toho.4010.name
    desc = {
        trigger = { NOT = {
            has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_country_selector
        } }
        text = "toho.4010.name"
    }
    desc = {
        trigger = { has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_country_selector has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_whitelist }
        text = "toho.4010.desc.whitelist"

    id = toho.4014
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = { has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_config_astral_gateway_acl_switch }

        remove_country_flag = touhou_gateway_config_astral_gateway_acl_switch

            remove_country_flag = touhou_country_selector_selected_country

                if = {
                    limit = { has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_whitelist NOT = { has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_allow_@prev } }
                    set_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_allow_@prev

                else_if = {
                    limit = { has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_blacklist NOT = { has_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_disallow_@prev } }
                    set_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_disallow_@prev
                }
            }
        }
    }

# on_megastructure_ownerless, gateway closed when ownerless.
system_event = {
    id = toho.4017
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        exists = fromfrom
        from = { is_megastructure_type = touhou_gateway_network_master_node }
    }
    immediate = {
        save_event_target_as = spth_target_system
        fromfrom = {
            country_event = { id = toho.4015 }
            touhou_gateway_master_node_flags_cleans_up_effect = { clear_global_event_target = yes }
        }
    }
}
# on_megastructure_change_owner, gateway owner change with control station.
country_event = {
    id = toho.4018
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = { from = { is_megastructure_type = touhou_gateway_network_master_node } }
    immediate = {
        from.solar_system = { save_event_target_as = spth_target_system }
        country_event = { id = toho.4016 }
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = fromfrom
                fromfrom = {
                    exists = event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller
                    is_same_value = event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller
                }
            }
            fromfrom = {
                country_event = { id = toho.4015 }
                touhou_gateway_master_node_flags_cleans_up_effect = { clear_global_event_target = yes }
            }
        } else_if = {
            limit = { exists = event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller }
            event_target:touhou_gateway_network_controller = {
                country_event = { id = toho.4015 }
                touhou_gateway_master_node_flags_cleans_up_effect = { clear_global_event_target = yes }
            }
        }
        save_global_event_target_as = touhou_gateway_network_controller
        set_country_flag = touhou_gateway_network_policy_blacklist
    }
}
fleet_event = {
    id = toho.4019
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        exists = this exists = owner exists = event_target:spth_deploy_gateway_system
        any_controlled_ship = { is_ship_size = touhou_makai_gateway_ship }
    }
    immediate = {
        random_controlled_ship = {
            limit = { is_ship_size = touhou_makai_gateway_ship }
            delete_ship = this
        }
        set_event_locked = no
        event_target:spth_deploy_gateway_system = {
            spawn_megastructure = {
                type = touhou_gateway_network_slave_node
                coords_from = root
                owner = root.owner
            }
        }
    }
}

        NOT = { has_country_flag = toho_4022_fired }

        check_modifier_value = { modifier = touhou_fallen_starship_makai_blocker_fixed value > 0 }
        has_technology = tech_housing_1
    }
    immediate = {

            limit = { NOT = { exists = event_target:touhou_origin_shell_world_@root } }
            random_owned_planet = {
                limit = { is_colony = yes has_planet_flag = origin_touhou_shell_world_system_planet }

        }

            set_planet_flag = touhou_origin_shell_world_triggered_story

    }
    option = {
        name = toho.4022.a
        default_hide_option = yes
        start_situation = {
            type = situation_touhou_shell_world_explore
            target = root
        }
    }

system_event = {
    id = toho.4202
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    immediate = {
        every_system_ambient_object = {
            limit = { has_ambient_object_flag = touhou_system_warper_attack_windip_in_range }
            destroy_ambient_object = this
        }
        random_system_planet = {
            limit = { is_primary_star = yes }
            planet_event = {
                id = toho.4204
                scopes = {
                    from = root
                }
            }
            event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = {
                country_event = {
                    id = toho.4203
                    scopes = {
                        from = event_target:spth_system_selector_target
                        fromfrom = root
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = space_owner
                space_owner = {
                    exists = this
                    is_touhou_common_country = yes
                    NOR = {
                        is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                        is_hostile = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                    }
                }
            }
            space_owner = {
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { has_communications = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user } }
                    establish_communications_no_message = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                }
                if = {
                    limit = { is_in_federation_with = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user }
                    leave_alliance = { override_requirements = yes apply_opinion_penalty = no }
                }
                country_event = { id = touhou_ship_actions.100 scopes = { from = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user } }
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { is_at_war_with = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user } }
                    event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { declare_war = { target = prev attacker_war_goal = "wg_spth_assimilation" } }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
