planet_event = {
    id = toho.4204
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = { is_star = yes }
    immediate = {
        event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = {
            save_event_target_as = touhou_target_country
            if = {
                limit = { any_owned_ship = { is_ship_class = shipclass_military } }
                random_controlled_ship = {
                    limit = { is_ship_class = shipclass_military }
                    save_event_target_as = touhou_target_ship
                }
            }
            else = { random_controlled_ship = { save_event_target_as = touhou_target_ship } }
        }
        event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = {
            switch = {
                trigger = galaxy_size
                huge = { add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 1000 } }
                large = { add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 1500 } }
                medium = { add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 2000 } }
                small = { add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 2500 } }
                tiny = { add_resource = { sr_dark_matter = 3000 } }
            }
        }
        solar_system = {
            set_asteroid_belt = { radius = 0 }
            every_system_planet = {
                limit = {
                    NOR = {
                        is_primary_star = yes
                        is_planet_class = pc_spth_void
                    }
                }
                prevent_anomaly = yes
                if = {
                    limit = { is_star = yes NOT = { is_planet_class = pc_black_hole } }
                    change_pc = pc_black_hole
                    set_planet_flag = touhou_new_void_star
                    set_variable = { which = touhou_planet_size value = trigger:planet_size }
                    multiply_variable = { which = touhou_planet_size value = 0.5 }
                    ceiling_variable = touhou_planet_size
                    set_planet_size = touhou_planet_size
                    clear_variable = touhou_planet_size
                    clear_deposits = yes
                    while = {
                        count = 3
                        random_list = {
                            75 = { }
                            50 = { add_deposit = d_dark_matter_deposit_1 }
                            50 = { add_deposit = d_dark_matter_deposit_2 }
                            50 = { add_deposit = d_dark_matter_deposit_3 }
                        }
                        random_list = {
                            100 = { }
                            25 = { add_deposit = d_physics_1 }
                            25 = { add_deposit = d_physics_2 }
                            25 = { add_deposit = d_physics_3 }
                            25 = { add_deposit = d_physics_4 }
                        }
                    }
                }
                else_if = {
                    limit = { is_asteroid = yes }
                    remove_planet = yes
                }
                else_if = {
                    limit = { is_planet_class = pc_gas_giant }
                    clear_deposits = yes
                    set_variable = { which = touhou_planet_size value = trigger:planet_size }
                    multiply_variable = { which = touhou_planet_size value = 0.75 }
                    ceiling_variable = touhou_planet_size
                    set_planet_size = touhou_planet_size
                    clear_variable = touhou_planet_size
                }
                else_if = {
                    limit = {
                        exists = owner
                        owner = { is_country_type = ag_zeta_crisis_country }
                    }
                    add_planet_devastation = 50
                }
                else_if = {
                    limit = { NOR = { is_planet_class = pc_shattered is_planet_class = pc_shattered_2 } }
                    clear_deposits = yes
                    if = {
                        limit = { has_observation_outpost = yes }
                        observation_outpost_owner = {
                            country_event = { id = planet_destruction.1000 days = 1 }
                        }
                    }
                    touhou_destroy_orbital_ring = { spawn_debris = no }
                    # Contingency Machine World
                    if = {
                        limit = { is_planet_class = pc_ai NOT = { has_planet_flag = machine_lair } }
                        set_planet_flag = destroyed_by_colossus
                        set_planet_flag = planet_cracked
                        add_planet_devastation = 50
                        planet_event = { id = crisis.2040 }
                    }
                    # Contingency Final Machine World
                    if = {
                        limit = { is_planet_class = pc_ai has_planet_flag = machine_lair }
                        set_planet_flag = destroyed_by_colossus
                        set_planet_flag = planet_cracked
                        event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { save_event_target_as = final_machine_world_destroyer }
                        stop_crisis_sound = yes
                        planet_event = { id = crisis.2046 }
                    }
                    # Swarm Situation Log counter
                    if = {
                        limit = { exists = owner owner = { is_country_type = swarm } }
                        every_country = {
                            limit = { has_event_chain = "prethoryn_scourge_chain" }
                            add_event_chain_counter = {
                                event_chain = "prethoryn_scourge_chain"
                                counter = "infested_worlds"
                                amount = -1
                            }
                            add_event_chain_counter = {
                                event_chain = "prethoryn_scourge_chain"
                                counter = "infested_worlds_cleansed"
                                amount = 1
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            OR = {
                                is_active_resolution = "resolution_rulesofwar_reverence_for_life"
                                is_active_resolution = "resolution_rulesofwar_independent_tribunals"
                                is_active_resolution = "resolution_rulesofwar_last_resort_doctrine"
                                is_active_resolution = "resolution_rulesofwar_demobilization_initiative"
                            }
                        }
                        event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { set_timed_country_flag = { flag = resolution_breached_fired_cracker days = 3600 } }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            exists = owner
                            owner = {
                                NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user }
                                is_touhou_common_country = yes
                            }
                        }
                        # Generate threat
                        if = {
                            limit = { is_colony = yes }
                            add_threat = { who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user amount = 3 }
                            # modifier for allies + those upset by genocide
                            every_country = {
                                limit = {
                                    NOR = {
                                        is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                        is_same_value = root.solar_system.space_owner
                                        AND = {
                                            has_federation = yes
                                            is_in_federation_with = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                        }
                                    }
                                    OR = {
                                        has_communications = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                        has_communications = root.solar_system.space_owner
                                    }
                                    OR = {
                                        AND = {
                                            has_federation = yes
                                            is_in_federation_with = root.solar_system.space_owner
                                        }
                                        has_ai_personality = awakened_fallen_empire_xenophile
                                        AND = {
                                            is_country_type = default
                                            OR = {
                                                is_egalitarian = yes
                                                is_xenophile = yes
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                if = {
                                    limit = { root = { is_colony = no } }
                                    add_opinion_modifier = {
                                        modifier = opinion_cracked_an_uninhabited_world
                                        who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                    }
                                }
                                else = {
                                    add_opinion_modifier = {
                                        modifier = opinion_cracked_a_world
                                        who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        else = { add_threat = { who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user amount = 1 } }
                        # modifiers for victim
                        owner = {
                            if = {
                                limit = { root = { is_colony = no } }
                                add_opinion_modifier = {
                                    modifier = opinion_cracked_my_uninhabited_world
                                    who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                }
                            }
                            else = {
                                add_opinion_modifier = {
                                    modifier = opinion_cracked_my_world
                                    who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                }
                            }
                            if = {
                                limit = { NOT = { has_ethic = ethic_gestalt_consciousness } }
                                add_modifier = { modifier = "colossus_victim" days = 10800 }
                            }
                        }
                        if = {
                            limit = { exists = owner owner = { is_country_type = primitive } }
                            # modifier for allies + those upset by genocide
                            every_country = {
                                limit = {
                                    NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user }
                                    OR = {
                                        has_communications = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                        has_communications = root.solar_system.space_owner
                                    }
                                    OR = {
                                        has_ai_personality = awakened_fallen_empire_xenophile
                                        AND = {
                                            is_country_type = default
                                            NOR = {
                                                is_xenophobe = yes
                                                is_homicidal = yes
                                                is_xenophile = yes
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                add_opinion_modifier = {
                                    modifier = opinion_cracked_a_primitive_world
                                    who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                }
                            }
                            every_country = {
                                limit = {
                                    NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user }
                                    OR = {
                                        has_communications = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                        has_communications = root.solar_system.space_owner
                                    }
                                    OR = {
                                        has_ai_personality = awakened_fallen_empire_xenophile
                                        AND = {
                                            is_country_type = default
                                            is_xenophile = yes
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                add_opinion_modifier = {
                                    modifier = opinion_cracked_a_primitive_world_phile
                                    who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { exists = owner }
                        owner = {
                            add_static_war_exhaustion = {
                                attacker = from.fleet.owner
                                location = root
                                value_for_planet_destruction = 1.0
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    clear_planet_modifiers = yes
                    remove_all_buildings = yes
                    # remove_all_districts = yes
                    if = { limit = { is_colony = yes } destroy_colony = yes }
                    if = {
                        limit = { OR = { touhou_is_habitat = yes is_planet_class = pc_habitat_shielded } }
                        spawn_habitat_cracker_effect = yes
                        solar_system.star = { remove_planet_flag = has_habitat }
                        remove_planet = yes
                    } else_if = {
                        limit = { is_planet_class = pc_cosmogenesis_world }
                        touhou_spawn_lathe_cracker_effect = yes
                    } else_if = {
                        limit = { OR = {
                            is_planet_class = pc_spiritual_power_habitat
                            has_planet_flag = is_spth_megastructure_planet
                        } }
                        spth_spawn_habitat_cracker_effect = yes
                    } else_if = {
                        limit = {
                            is_ringworld = yes
                            NOR = {
                                is_planet_class = pc_ag_ringworld_shrouded
                                is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_habitable_damaged
                                is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_seam
                                is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_seam_damaged
                                is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_tech
                                is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_tech_damaged
                            }
                        }
                        spawn_ringworld_cracker_effect = yes
                        touhou_destroy_habitable_ringworld = yes
                    } else_if = {
                        limit = { is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_seam }
                        spawn_ringworld_cracker_effect = yes
                        change_pc = pc_ringworld_seam_damaged
                    } else_if = {
                        limit = { is_planet_class = pc_ringworld_tech }
                        spawn_ringworld_cracker_effect = yes
                        change_pc = pc_ringworld_tech_damaged
                    }
                    else_if = {
                        limit = {
                            NOR = {
                                touhou_is_habitat = yes
                                is_ringworld = yes
                                is_planet_class = pc_habitat_shielded
                            }
                        }
                        change_pc = pc_shattered
                        if = {
                            limit = {
                                NOR = {
                                    is_planet_class = pc_molten
                                    is_planet_class = pc_toxic
                                    is_planet_class = pc_barren
                                    is_planet_class = pc_barren_cold
                                    is_planet_class = pc_frozen
                                }
                            }
                            random_list = {
                                25 = { while = { count = 4 add_deposit = d_minerals_4 } }
                                25 = { while = { count = 3 add_deposit = d_minerals_4 } }
                                25 = { while = { count = 2 add_deposit = d_minerals_4 } }
                                25 = { add_deposit = d_minerals_4 }
                            }
                            reroll_random = yes
                        }
                        else = {
                            while = {
                                count = 2
                                random_list = {
                                    100 = { }
                                    25 = { add_deposit = d_minerals_1 }
                                    25 = { add_deposit = d_minerals_2 }
                                    25 = { add_deposit = d_minerals_3 }
                                    25 = { add_deposit = d_minerals_4 }
                                }
                                reroll_random = yes
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            solar_system = { has_star_flag = sol }
                            event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { NOT = { species_portrait = human } }
                            OR = {
                                has_planet_flag = planet_earth
                                has_planet_flag = nuked_earth
                                AND = {
                                    exists = owner
                                    owner = {
                                        is_primitive = yes
                                        species_portrait = human
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        random_list = {
                            50 = { }
                            50 = {
                                solar_system = { save_event_target_as = sol_sys }
                                event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { country_event = { id = planet_destruction.111 } }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = { has_modifier = "holy_planet" }
                        change_pc = pc_shattered
                        remove_modifier = "holy_planet"
                    }
                    event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = {
                        if = {
                            limit = { has_ascension_perk = ap_become_the_crisis }
                            complete_crisis_objective = crisobj_destroy_worlds
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { exists = starbase }
                starbase.fleet = {
                    every_controlled_ship = {
                        destroy_ship = this
                        fire_on_action = {
                            on_action = on_ship_disabled
                            scopes = { from = event_target:touhou_target_ship }
                        }
                        owner = {
                            fire_on_action = {
                                on_action = on_ship_destroyed_victim
                                scopes = {
                                    from = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                    fromfrom = prev
                                    fromfromfrom = event_target:touhou_target_ship
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = {
                            fire_on_action = {
                                on_action = on_ship_destroyed_perp
                                scopes = {
                                    from = prev.owner
                                    fromfrom = event_target:touhou_target_ship
                                    fromfromfrom = prev
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    owner = {
                        fire_on_action = {
                            on_action = on_fleet_destroyed_victim
                            scopes = {
                                from = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                fromfrom = prev
                                fromfromfrom = root.from
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = {
                        fire_on_action = {
                            on_action = on_fleet_destroyed_perp
                            scopes = {
                                from = prev.owner
                                fromfrom = root.from
                                fromfromfrom = prev
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            every_fleet_in_system = {
                limit = {
                    exists = this
                    NAND = { prev = { exists = starbase } is_same_value = prev.starbase.fleet }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        exists = owner
                        owner = {
                            OR = {
                                is_touhou_crisis_faction = yes
                                is_invisable_faction = yes
                                ag_is_ancient_country = yes
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    reroll_random = yes
                    every_controlled_ship = {
                        spth_reduce_shield_max = { percent = 1 }
                        spth_reduce_armor_max = { percent = 1 }
                    }
                } else = {
                    every_controlled_ship = {
                        destroy_ship = this
                        fire_on_action = {
                            on_action = on_ship_disabled
                            scopes = { from = event_target:touhou_target_ship }
                        }
                        owner = { fire_on_action = {
                            on_action = on_ship_destroyed_victim
                            scopes = {
                                from = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                                fromfrom = prev
                                fromfromfrom = event_target:touhou_target_ship
                            }
                        } }
                        event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { fire_on_action = {
                            on_action = on_ship_destroyed_perp
                            scopes = {
                                from = prev.owner
                                fromfromfrom = prev
                                fromfrom = event_target:touhou_target_ship
                            }
                        } }
                    }
                    owner = { fire_on_action = {
                        on_action = on_fleet_destroyed_victim
                        scopes = {
                            from = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                            fromfrom = prev
                            fromfromfrom = root.from
                        }
                    } }
                    event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user = { fire_on_action = {
                        on_action = on_fleet_destroyed_perp
                        scopes = {
                            from = prev.owner
                            fromfrom = root.from
                            fromfromfrom = prev
                        }
                    } }
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { NOT = { exists = space_owner } }
                every_country = {
                    limit = {
                        NOR = {
                            is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                            is_country_type = fallen_empire
                        }
                        has_communications = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                    }
                    add_opinion_modifier = {
                        modifier = touhou_opinion_destroyed_system
                        who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                    }
                }
            }
            else = { every_country = {
                limit = {
                    NOR = {
                        is_same_value = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                        is_country_type = fallen_empire
                    }
                    OR = {
                        has_communications = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                        has_communications = prev.space_owner
                    }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = { is_same_value = prev.space_owner }
                    add_opinion_modifier = {
                        modifier = touhou_opinion_destroyed_my_system
                        who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                    }
                }
                else = {
                    add_opinion_modifier = {
                        modifier = touhou_opinion_destroyed_system
                        who = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                    }
                }
            } }
        }
        every_country = {
            create_message = {
                type = "STAR_DESTROYED"
                localization = "message_touhou_supercarrier_pk_weapon_killed_star_desc"
                days = 30
                target = root
                variable = {
                    type = name
                    localization = "touhou_target_planet"
                    scope = root
                }
                variable = {
                    type = name
                    localization = "touhou_target_system"
                    scope = root.solar_system
                }
                variable = {
                    type = name
                    localization = "touhou_attacker"
                    scope = event_target:touhou_system_warper_attack_user
                }
                variable = {
                    type = name
                    localization = "touhou_attacker_ship"
                    scope = from
                }
            }
        }
        create_ambient_object = { type = "destroyed_system" location = this }
        last_created_ambient_object = {
            set_ambient_object_flag = destroyed_system_effect
            set_location = { target = root distance = 0 angle = random }
        }
        th_spawn_explosion_particle = { size = large color = pink }
    }
    after = {
        change_pc = pc_spth_void
        solar_system = { set_star_flag = star_cracked set_star_class = sc_spth_void }
        set_variable = { which = touhou_planet_size value = trigger:planet_size }
        multiply_variable = { which = touhou_planet_size value = 0.5 }
        ceiling_variable = touhou_planet_size
        set_planet_size = touhou_planet_size
        clear_variable = touhou_planet_size
        prevent_anomaly = yes
        clear_deposits = yes
    }
}

# still controls the system warper when ownerless.
# Executed when a megastructure is rendered ownerless
# this = solar system containing megastructure
# from = megastructure
# fromfrom = old owner (if existing)
system_event = {
    id = toho.4209
    is_triggered_only = yes
    hide_window = yes
    trigger = {
        exists = fromfrom
        from = {
            OR = {
                is_megastructure_type = touhou_system_warper
                is_megastructure_type = touhou_system_warper_self_jump
                is_megastructure_type = touhou_system_warper_system_jump
                is_megastructure_type = touhou_system_warper_attack
            }
        }
    }
    immediate = {
        from = { set_owner = root.fromfrom }
    }
}

        set_country_flag = tech_touhou_makai_wedge_titan_petascale

    diplomatic = yes

        touhou_give_tech_option = { touhou_tech = tech_touhou_common_bow research_point = yes progress = 0.2 }
    }
}

    }

country_event = {
	id = toho.5000
	title = BUTTON_TEXT_NULL
	desc = BUTTON_TEXT_NULL
	is_triggered_only = yes
	diplomatic = yes
	custom_gui = spth_offical_comics_ui
	custom_gui_option = spth_offical_comics_button
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_variable_set = spth_offical_comics_page } }
			set_variable = { which = spth_offical_comics_page value = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_variable_set = spth_offical_comics_page_max } }
			set_variable = { which = spth_offical_comics_page_max value = 29 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = toho.5000.prev
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = spth_offical_comics_page value > 0 } }
				change_variable = { which = spth_offical_comics_page value = -1 }
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = toho.5000 }
	}
	option = {
		name = toho.5000.next
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { which = spth_offical_comics_page value < spth_offical_comics_page_max } }
				change_variable = { which = spth_offical_comics_page value = 1 }
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = toho.5000 }
	}
	option = {
		name = gensokyo_start.1147.exit
		default_hide_option = yes
	}
}

@touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_stage_1_progress = 250
@touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_stage_2_progress = 500
@touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_stage_3_progress = 750
situation_event = {
    id = toho.5011
    title = toho.5011.name
    desc = toho.5011.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        NOT = { has_situation_flag = toho_5011_fired }
        has_situation_flag = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_living_metal
        situation_progress >= @[touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_stage_1_progress + 50]
    }
    immediate = { set_situation_flag = toho_5011_fired }
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_resource_cache_room }
    option = {
        name = toho.5011.a
        allow = {
            owner = {
                resource_stockpile_compare = {
                    resource = sr_living_metal
                    value >= 3600
                }
            }
        }
        owner = { add_resource = { sr_living_metal = -3600 } }
        hidden_effect = {
            set_timed_situation_flag = { flag = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_living_metal_sample days = 360 }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.5011.b
        allow = { NOT = { current_situation_approach = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_approach_positive } }
        set_situation_approach = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_approach_positive
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.5011.c
        allow = { NOT = { current_situation_approach = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_approach_netual } }
        set_situation_approach = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_approach_netual
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.5011.d
        allow = { NOT = { current_situation_approach = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_approach_negative } }
        set_situation_approach = touhou_rare_resourse_analysis_approach_negative
    }
}

    } }
    option = {

    }
    option = {

    }
}

# on_ship_destroyed_perp, Kill Grand Archive.
country_event = {
    id = toho.10012
    title = toho.10012.name
    desc = toho.10012.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_small_space_battle_room }
    show_sound = event_space_battle
    location = root.solar_system
    trigger = {
        exists = event_target:touhou_the_grand_archive_ship_country
        from = {
            is_same_value = event_target:touhou_the_grand_archive_ship_country
            count_controlled_ship = { count < 1 }
        }
        fromfromfrom = { has_ship_flag = touhou_kedama_the_grand_archive }
    }
    immediate = {
        set_global_flag = touhou_the_grand_archive_ship_destroyed
        fromfromfrom = {
            create_ambient_object = {
                location = this
                type = habitat_cracker_object
                play_animation_once = yes
                duration = 5
                use_3d_location = yes
                base_angle_towards = star
                entity_face_object = star
                entity_offset = { min = 0 max = 0 }
                entity_scale_to_size = yes
                scale = 0.125
            }
            create_ambient_object = {
                location = this
                type = large_debris_object
                play_animation_once = yes
                use_3d_location = yes
                base_angle_towards = star
                entity_face_object = star
                entity_offset = { min = 0 max = 0 }
                entity_scale_to_size = yes
                effect = {
                    set_ambient_object_flag = touhou_the_grand_archive_ship_wreckage
                    save_global_event_target_as = touhou_the_grand_archive_ship_wreckage
                }
            }
            # solar_system = { spawn_megastructure = {
            #     type = grand_archive_0
            #     coords_from = root.fromfromfrom
            #     init_effect = {
            #     }
            # } }
        }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.10012.a
        event_target:touhou_the_grand_archive_ship_wreckage = { enable_special_project = {
            name = "touhou_kedama_the_grand_archive_project"
            location = this
            owner = root
        } }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.10012.b
        owner = { add_resource = { influence = 100 } }
    }
}

country_event = {
    id = toho.10031
    title = toho.10031.name
    desc = toho.10031.desc
    is_triggered_only = yes
    diplomatic = yes
    custom_gui = spth_event_large_window
    custom_gui_option = spth_event_large_ui_option
    picture_event_data = { room = spth_skrand_ship_room }
    immediate = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = { exists = event_target:touhou_makai_netural }
            }
            create_country = {
                name = touhou_makai_netural
                type = touhou_makai_netural
                flag = {
                    icon = {
                        category = "touhou_fe_union"
                        file = "th_ag_fallen_empires_1.dds"
                    }
                    colors = {
                        "sky_blue"
                        "sky_blue"
                        "sky_blue"
                        "null"
                    }
                    background = {
                        category = "backgrounds"
                        file = "00_solid.dds"
                    }
                }
                name_list = random
                effect = {
                    set_graphical_culture = strg_01
                    set_country_flag = touhou_mod_country
                    set_country_flag = touhou_crisis_country
                    set_country_flag = touhou_makai_netural
                    set_country_flag = protected_from_queen_storm
                    add_modifier = { modifier = touhou_immune_from_terrify }
                    save_global_event_target_as = touhou_makai_netural
                    every_playable_country = {
                        limit = { NOT = { is_same_value = prev } }
                        establish_communications_no_message = prev
                        add_intel = { who = prev amount = 70 }
                    }
                    every_country = {
                        limit = {
                            OR = {
                                is_country_type = default
                                is_country_type = fallen_empire
                                is_country_type = awakened_fallen_empire
                            }
                            NOT = { has_communications = prev }
                        }
                        establish_communications_no_message = prev
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        event_target:touhou_makai_netural = {
            spth_effect_init_gengetsu_effect = yes
        }
        event_target:spth_precursor_makai_network_system = {
            random_system_megastructure = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        is_megastructure_type = touhou_gateway_network_master_node_damaged
                        is_megastructure_type = touhou_gateway_network_master_node_locked
                        is_megastructure_type = touhou_gateway_network_master_node
                        is_megastructure_type = touhou_gateway_network_master_node_configure
                    }
                }
                create_fleet = {
                    name = NAME_touhou_sky_control_fleet
                    settings = { can_disband = no can_change_composition = no can_change_leader = no spawn_debris = no }
                    effect = {
                        set_owner = event_target:touhou_makai_netural
                        set_location = prev
                        while = {
                            count = 1
                            create_ship = {
                                fleet_cache_recalc = no
                                design = NAME_touhou_sky_control_ship
                                effect = {
                                    set_ship_flag = touhou_sky_control_ship_event
                                    save_global_event_target_as = touhou_sky_control_ship_event_target
                                    touhou_difficulty_bonus_ship = yes
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        set_fleet_flag = NAME_touhou_sky_control_fleet
                        set_fleet_flag = touhou_makai_netural
                        assign_leader = event_target:touhou_leader_temp_last_created
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        country_event = { id = toho.10032 days = 60 random = 60 }
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.10031.a
    }
    option = {
        name = toho.10031.b
    }
}

# Final Speech - Shroud Ending
country_event = {
    id = toho.23020
    title = "toho.23020.name"
    diplomatic_title = "toho.23020.name"
    desc = "toho.23020.desc"
    show_sound = crisis_23020_desc

    diplomatic = yes

    event_window_type = crisis_leader_conversation
    situation = event_target:synth_queen_doomclock_situation

    picture_event_data = {
        portrait = event_target:synth_queen_country.ruler
        room = event_target:synth_queen_country.owner
    }
    picture = GFX_crisis_conversation_bg_synth_queen

    is_triggered_only = yes

    immediate = {
        create_species = {
            name = "NAME_Cetana"
            class = "ROBOT"
            namelist = "CETANA"
            portrait = "synth_queen"
            traits = {
                trait = "trait_mechanical"
            }
            effect = {
                save_event_target_as = synth_queen_species
            }
        }
        create_country = {
            name = "NAME_Synth_Queen"
            type = "synth_queen"
            species = event_target:synth_queen_species
            name_list = "CETANA"
            flag = {
                icon = {
                    category = "special"
                    file = "synth_queen.dds"
                }
                background = {
                    category = "backgrounds"
                    file = "flag_BG_13.dds"
                }
                colors = {
                    "burgundy"
                    "burgundy"
                    "null"
                    "null"
                }
            }
            effect = {
                save_event_target_as = synth_queen_country
                establish_communications = root
                create_leader = {
                    class = commander
                    species = event_target:synth_queen_country
                    name = "NAME_Cetana"
                    immortal = yes
                    skill = 10
                    event_leader = yes
                    skip_background_generation = yes
                    set_age = 126872
                    custom_description = cetana_desc
                    traits = {
                        0 = subclass_commander_councilor
                    }
                    effect = {
                        change_leader_portrait = cetana_empty
                    }
                }
                set_leader = last_created_leader
            }
        }
    }

    #Reshaped
    option = {
        name = crisis.23020.a
        is_dialog_only = yes
        response_text = toho.23020.a.reply
        sound = crisis_23020_1_reply
        trigger = {
            NOT = {
                has_country_flag = toho.crisis.8295.1.flag
            }
        }
        hidden_effect = {
            set_country_flag = toho.crisis.8295.1.flag
        }
    }

    option = {
        name = crisis.23020.1.a
        custom_tooltip = crisis.23020.1.a.tt
        sound = crisis_23020_1_reply
        trigger = {
            has_country_flag = toho.crisis.8295.1.flag
        }
        hidden_effect = {
            remove_country_flag = toho.crisis.8295.1.flag
        }
    }

    after = {
        defeating_synth_queen_rewards = yes
        hidden_effect = {
            every_country = {
                limit = {
                    is_synth_queen_country_type = yes
                }
                destroy_country = yes
            }
            every_country = {
                remove_country_flag = synth_queen_player_can_see_doomclock
            }
            every_country = {
                limit = {
                    has_modifier = queenexecutioner
                }
                remove_modifier = queenexecutioner
            }
            every_country = {
                limit = {
                    has_modifier = queenslayer
                }
                remove_modifier = queenslayer
            }
            every_country = {
                limit = {
                    NOT = { is_same_value = event_target:touhou_defeated_synth_queen }
                }
                country_event = {
                    id = toho.23030
                    days = 15
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

# Cetana Has Been Defeated - Delete Outpost Countries and Other Settings.
event = {
    id = toho.23031
    hide_window = yes
    is_triggered_only = yes
    trigger = {
        NOT = { has_global_flag = toho_23031_fired }
        has_global_flag = synth_queen_defeated
    }
    immediate = {
        set_global_flag = toho_23031_fired
        set_update_modifiers_batch = begin
        # Outposts: dismantled
        every_country = {
            limit = { is_country_type = synth_queen_outposts }
            every_owned_fleet = {
                delete_fleet = {
                    target = this
                    kill_leader = yes
                    destroy_template = yes
                }
            }
            destroy_country = yes
        }
        # Convoys: go home
        every_country = {
            limit = { is_country_type = synth_queen_convoys }
            every_owned_fleet = {
                delete_fleet = {
                    target = this
                    kill_leader = yes
                    destroy_template = yes
                }
            }
            destroy_country = yes
        }
        # Destroy every situation
        every_country = {
            limit = { any_situation = { is_situation_type = situation_synth_queen_player_facing } }
            every_situation = {
                limit = { is_situation_type = situation_synth_queen_player_facing }
                destroy_situation = this
            }
        }
        set_update_modifiers_batch = end
    }
}
